TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Parallel similarity search across multiple queries\n\nAchieves 1.5x speedup for indexing and 2x speedup for searching with 4 workers.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\n\nfrom sequential import (\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    SearchResult,\n    IndexingResult,\n    tokenize,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    STOP_WORDS,\n)\nfrom document_generator import Document\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Indexing Result\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions for Parallel Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef process_document_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Process a chunk of documents to compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (chunk of documents, chunk_id)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents, chunk_id = args\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n\n\ndef compute_document_frequencies(args):\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a subset of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_subset, doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary of term -> document frequency\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_subset, doc_terms = args\n    local_df = {}\n    for term in terms_subset:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        local_df[term] = df\n    return local_df\n\n\ndef build_posting_list(args):\n    \"\"\"Build posting list for a single term.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (term, doc_term_freqs, idf_score)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (term, posting_list)\n    \"\"\"\n    term, doc_term_freqs, idf_score = args\n    posting_list = []\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        if term in tf_dict:\n            tf = tf_dict[term]\n            tfidf = tf * idf_score\n            posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n    # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n    posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    return (term, posting_list)\n\n\ndef compute_doc_vector_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Compute document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors_dict, doc_norms_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        if doc_id not in doc_term_freqs:\n            continue\n\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            if term in idf:\n                tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return (doc_vectors, doc_norms)\n\n\ndef search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef search_batch_queries(args):\n    \"\"\"Search a batch of queries.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (batch_queries, index, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists\n    \"\"\"\n    batch_queries, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n    batch_results = []\n    for query in batch_queries:\n        result = search_single_query((query, index, top_k, doc_titles))\n        batch_results.append(result)\n    return batch_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents: list[Document], num_workers=None, chunk_size=1000) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for terms in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores in parallel\n    5. Compute document vector norms in parallel\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 1000)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Use a single Pool to reduce overhead\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        # Step 1: Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n        chunks = [documents[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)]\n        chunk_args = [(chunk, i) for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)]\n\n        results = pool.map(process_document_chunk, chunk_args)\n\n        # Merge results from all chunks\n        doc_term_freqs = {}\n        doc_terms = {}\n        vocabulary = set()\n\n        for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_doc_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n            vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n        index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n        # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n        vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n        vocab_chunk_size = max(100, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n        vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i:i + vocab_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size)]\n        df_args = [(vocab_chunk, doc_terms) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n        df_results = pool.map(compute_document_frequencies, df_args)\n\n        # Merge DF results\n        for df_dict in df_results:\n            index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Use a single Pool for the remaining steps\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        # Step 4: Build inverted index in parallel\n        idf = index.idf\n        # Process terms in larger chunks to reduce overhead\n        terms_list = list(vocabulary)\n        term_chunk_size = max(50, len(terms_list) // (num_workers * 4))\n        term_chunks = [terms_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(terms_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n        posting_results = []\n        for term_chunk in term_chunks:\n            chunk_args = [(term, doc_term_freqs, idf[term]) for term in term_chunk]\n            chunk_results = pool.map(build_posting_list, chunk_args)\n            posting_results.extend(chunk_results)\n\n        # Merge posting lists\n        for term, posting_list in posting_results:\n            index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n        # Step 5: Compute document vectors and norms in parallel\n        doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n        doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n        doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n        vector_args = [(doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf) for doc_chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n        vector_results = pool.map(compute_doc_vector_chunk, vector_args)\n\n        # Merge vector results\n        for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vector_results:\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries: list[str], index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int = 10, num_workers=None, documents=None) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Batch queries to reduce multiprocessing overhead\n    if len(queries) <= num_workers:\n        # If we have fewer queries than workers, use one query per worker\n        search_args = [(query, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]\n        with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n            results = pool.map(search_single_query, search_args)\n    else:\n        # Batch queries - process multiple queries per worker\n        batch_size = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n        query_batches = [queries[i:i + batch_size] for i in range(0, len(queries), batch_size)]\n\n        def search_batch(batch_queries):\n            \"\"\"Search a batch of queries.\"\"\"\n            batch_results = []\n            for query in batch_queries:\n                result = search_single_query((query, index, top_k, doc_titles))\n                batch_results.append(result)\n            return batch_results\n\n        search_args = [query_batch for query_batch in query_batches]\n\n        with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n            batch_results = pool.map(search_batch, search_args)\n\n        # Flatten batch results\n        results = []\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            results.extend(batch)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of worker processes\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    from sequential import load_corpus, generate_corpus\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for terms in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores in parallel\n    5. Compute document vector norms in parallel\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 1000)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Use a single Pool to reduce overhead\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        # Step 1: Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n        chunks = [documents[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)]\n        chunk_args = [(chunk, i) for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)]\n\n        results = pool.map(process_document_chunk, chunk_args)\n\n        # Merge results from all chunks\n        doc_term_freqs = {}\n        doc_terms = {}\n        vocabulary = set()\n\n        for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_doc_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n            vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n        index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n        # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n        vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n        vocab_chunk_size = max(100, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n        vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i:i + vocab_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size)]\n        df_args = [(vocab_chunk, doc_terms) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n        df_results = pool.map(compute_document_frequencies, df_args)\n\n        # Merge DF results\n        for df_dict in df_results:\n            index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Use a single Pool for the remaining steps\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        # Step 4: Build inverted index in parallel\n        idf = index.idf\n        # Process terms in larger chunks to reduce overhead\n        terms_list = list(vocabulary)\n        term_chunk_size = max(50, len(terms_list) // (num_workers * 4))\n        term_chunks = [terms_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(terms_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n        posting_results = []\n        for term_chunk in term_chunks:\n            chunk_args = [(term, doc_term_freqs, idf[term]) for term in term_chunk]\n            chunk_results = pool.map(build_posting_list, chunk_args)\n            posting_results.extend(chunk_results)\n\n        # Merge posting lists\n        for term, posting_list in posting_results:\n            index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n        # Step 5: Compute document vectors and norms in parallel\n        doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n        doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n        doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n        vector_args = [(doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf) for doc_chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n        vector_results = pool.map(compute_doc_vector_chunk, vector_args)\n\n        # Merge vector results\n        for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vector_results:\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for terms in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores in parallel\n    5. Compute document vector norms in parallel\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 1000)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Create pool once and reuse\n    pool = mp.Pool(processes=num_workers)\n\n    try:\n        # Step 1: Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n        chunks = [documents[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)]\n        chunk_args = [(chunk, i) for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)]\n\n        results = pool.map(process_document_chunk, chunk_args)\n\n        # Merge results from all chunks\n        doc_term_freqs = {}\n        doc_terms = {}\n        vocabulary = set()\n\n        for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_doc_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n            vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n        index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n        # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n        vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n        vocab_chunk_size = max(100, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n        vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i:i + vocab_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size)]\n        df_args = [(vocab_chunk, doc_terms) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n        df_results = pool.map(compute_document_frequencies, df_args)\n\n        # Merge DF results\n        for df_dict in df_results:\n            index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n        # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n        N = index.num_documents\n        for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n            index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n        # Step 4: Build inverted index in parallel\n        idf = index.idf\n        # Process terms in larger chunks to reduce overhead\n        terms_list = list(vocabulary)\n        term_chunk_size = max(50, len(terms_list) // (num_workers * 4))\n        term_chunks = [terms_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(terms_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n        posting_results = []\n        for term_chunk in term_chunks:\n            chunk_args = [(term, doc_term_freqs, idf[term]) for term in term_chunk]\n            chunk_results = pool.map(build_posting_list, chunk_args)\n            posting_results.extend(chunk_results)\n\n        # Merge posting lists\n        for term, posting_list in posting_results:\n            index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n        # Step 5: Compute document vectors and norms in parallel\n        doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n        doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n        doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n        vector_args = [(doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf) for doc_chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n        vector_results = pool.map(compute_doc_vector_chunk, vector_args)\n\n        # Merge vector results\n        for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vector_results:\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n    finally:\n        pool.close()\n        pool.join()\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":561,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1712,"cache_read_input_tokens":39869},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8b6d14d9-3833-48e0-93e2-66fec3c6b7e6","uuid":"1edf2205-3402-4103-a741-8f765b9f6df3"}

Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for terms in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores in parallel\n    5. Compute document vector norms in parallel\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 1000)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Create pool once and reuse\n    pool = mp.Pool(processes=num_workers)\n\n    try:\n        # Step 1: Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n        chunks = [documents[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)]\n        chunk_args = [(chunk, i) for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)]\n\n        results = pool.map(process_document_chunk, chunk_args)\n\n        # Merge results from all chunks\n        doc_term_freqs = {}\n        doc_terms = {}\n        vocabulary = set()\n\n        for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_doc_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n            vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n        index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n        # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n        vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n        vocab_chunk_size = max(100, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n        vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i:i + vocab_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size)]\n        df_args = [(vocab_chunk, doc_terms) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n        df_results = pool.map(compute_document_frequencies, df_args)\n\n        # Merge DF results\n        for df_dict in df_results:\n            index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n        # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n        N = index.num_documents\n        for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n            index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n        # Step 4: Build inverted index in parallel\n        idf = index.idf\n        # Process terms in larger chunks to reduce overhead\n        terms_list = list(vocabulary)\n        term_chunk_size = max(50, len(terms_list) // (num_workers * 4))\n        term_chunks = [terms_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(terms_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n        posting_results = []\n        for term_chunk in term_chunks:\n            chunk_args = [(term, doc_term_freqs, idf[term]) for term in term_chunk]\n            chunk_results = pool.map(build_posting_list, chunk_args)\n            posting_results.extend(chunk_results)\n\n        # Merge posting lists\n        for term, posting_list in posting_results:\n            index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n        # Step 5: Compute document vectors and norms in parallel\n        doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n        doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n        doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n        vector_args = [(doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf) for doc_chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n        vector_results = pool.map(compute_doc_vector_chunk, vector_args)\n\n        # Merge vector results\n        for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vector_results:\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n    finally:\n        pool.close()\n        pool.join()\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Parallel similarity search across multiple queries\n\nAchieves 1.5x speedup for indexing and 2x speedup for searching with 4 workers.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as IndexingResult,\n    tokenize,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    STOP_WORDS,\n)\nfrom document_generator import Document\n\n# TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions for Parallel Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef process_document_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Process a chunk of documents to compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (chunk of documents, chunk_id)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents, chunk_id = args\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n\n\ndef compute_document_frequencies(args):\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a subset of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_subset, doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary of term -> document frequency\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_subset, doc_terms = args\n    local_df = {}\n    for term in terms_subset:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        local_df[term] = df\n    return local_df\n\n\ndef build_posting_list(args):\n    \"\"\"Build posting list for a single term.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (term, doc_term_freqs, idf_score)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (term, posting_list)\n    \"\"\"\n    term, doc_term_freqs, idf_score = args\n    posting_list = []\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        if term in tf_dict:\n            tf = tf_dict[term]\n            tfidf = tf * idf_score\n            posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n    # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n    posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    return (term, posting_list)\n\n\ndef compute_doc_vector_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Compute document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors_dict, doc_norms_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        if doc_id not in doc_term_freqs:\n            continue\n\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            if term in idf:\n                tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return (doc_vectors, doc_norms)\n\n\ndef search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef search_batch_queries(args):\n    \"\"\"Search a batch of queries.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (batch_queries, index, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists\n    \"\"\"\n    batch_queries, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n    batch_results = []\n    for query in batch_queries:\n        result = search_single_query((query, index, top_k, doc_titles))\n        batch_results.append(result)\n    return batch_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents: list[Document], num_workers=None, chunk_size=1000) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for terms in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores in parallel\n    5. Compute document vector norms in parallel\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 1000)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Use a single Pool to reduce overhead\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        # Step 1: Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n        chunks = [documents[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)]\n        chunk_args = [(chunk, i) for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)]\n\n        results = pool.map(process_document_chunk, chunk_args)\n\n        # Merge results from all chunks\n        doc_term_freqs = {}\n        doc_terms = {}\n        vocabulary = set()\n\n        for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_doc_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n            vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n        index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n        # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n        vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n        vocab_chunk_size = max(100, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n        vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i:i + vocab_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size)]\n        df_args = [(vocab_chunk, doc_terms) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n        df_results = pool.map(compute_document_frequencies, df_args)\n\n        # Merge DF results\n        for df_dict in df_results:\n            index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Use a single Pool for the remaining steps\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        # Step 4: Build inverted index in parallel\n        idf = index.idf\n        # Process terms in larger chunks to reduce overhead\n        terms_list = list(vocabulary)\n        term_chunk_size = max(50, len(terms_list) // (num_workers * 4))\n        term_chunks = [terms_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(terms_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n        posting_results = []\n        for term_chunk in term_chunks:\n            chunk_args = [(term, doc_term_freqs, idf[term]) for term in term_chunk]\n            chunk_results = pool.map(build_posting_list, chunk_args)\n            posting_results.extend(chunk_results)\n\n        # Merge posting lists\n        for term, posting_list in posting_results:\n            index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n        # Step 5: Compute document vectors and norms in parallel\n        doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n        doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n        doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n        vector_args = [(doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf) for doc_chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n        vector_results = pool.map(compute_doc_vector_chunk, vector_args)\n\n        # Merge vector results\n        for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vector_results:\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries: list[str], index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int = 10, num_workers=None, documents=None) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Batch queries to reduce multiprocessing overhead\n    if len(queries) <= num_workers:\n        # If we have fewer queries than workers, use one query per worker\n        search_args = [(query, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]\n        with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n            results = pool.map(search_single_query, search_args)\n    else:\n        # Batch queries - process multiple queries per worker\n        batch_size = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n        query_batches = [queries[i:i + batch_size] for i in range(0, len(queries), batch_size)]\n\n        search_args = [(query_batch, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query_batch in query_batches]\n\n        with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n            batch_results = pool.map(search_batch_queries, search_args)\n\n        # Flatten batch results\n        results = []\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            results.extend(batch)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of worker processes\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    from sequential import load_corpus, generate_corpus\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Parallel similarity search across multiple queries\n\nAchieves 1.5x speedup for indexing and 2x speedup for searching with 4 workers.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\n\nfrom sequential import (\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    SearchResult,\n    IndexingResult,\n    tokenize,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    STOP_WORDS,\n)\nfrom document_generator import Document\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Indexing Result\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions for Parallel Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef process_document_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Process a chunk of documents to compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (chunk of documents, chunk_id)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary_set)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents, chunk_id = args\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n\n\ndef compute_document_frequencies(args):\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a subset of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_subset, doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary of term -> document frequency\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_subset, doc_terms = args\n    local_df = {}\n    for term in terms_subset:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        local_df[term] = df\n    return local_df\n\n\ndef build_posting_list(args):\n    \"\"\"Build posting list for a single term.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (term, doc_term_freqs, idf_score)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (term, posting_list)\n    \"\"\"\n    term, doc_term_freqs, idf_score = args\n    posting_list = []\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n        if term in tf_dict:\n            tf = tf_dict[term]\n            tfidf = tf * idf_score\n            posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n    # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n    posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n    return (term, posting_list)\n\n\ndef compute_doc_vector_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Compute document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors_dict, doc_norms_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        if doc_id not in doc_term_freqs:\n            continue\n\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            if term in idf:\n                tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return (doc_vectors, doc_norms)\n\n\ndef search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef search_batch_queries(args):\n    \"\"\"Search a batch of queries.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (batch_queries, index, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists\n    \"\"\"\n    batch_queries, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n    batch_results = []\n    for query in batch_queries:\n        result = search_single_query((query, index, top_k, doc_titles))\n        batch_results.append(result)\n    return batch_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents: list[Document], num_workers=None, chunk_size=1000) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for terms in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores in parallel\n    5. Compute document vector norms in parallel\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 1000)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Create pool once and reuse\n    pool = mp.Pool(processes=num_workers)\n\n    try:\n        # Step 1: Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n        chunks = [documents[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)]\n        chunk_args = [(chunk, i) for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)]\n\n        results = pool.map(process_document_chunk, chunk_args)\n\n        # Merge results from all chunks\n        doc_term_freqs = {}\n        doc_terms = {}\n        vocabulary = set()\n\n        for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_doc_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n            vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n        index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n        # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n        vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n        vocab_chunk_size = max(100, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n        vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i:i + vocab_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size)]\n        df_args = [(vocab_chunk, doc_terms) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n        df_results = pool.map(compute_document_frequencies, df_args)\n\n        # Merge DF results\n        for df_dict in df_results:\n            index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n        # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n        N = index.num_documents\n        for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n            index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n        # Step 4: Build inverted index in parallel\n        idf = index.idf\n        # Process terms in larger chunks to reduce overhead\n        terms_list = list(vocabulary)\n        term_chunk_size = max(50, len(terms_list) // (num_workers * 4))\n        term_chunks = [terms_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(terms_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n        posting_results = []\n        for term_chunk in term_chunks:\n            chunk_args = [(term, doc_term_freqs, idf[term]) for term in term_chunk]\n            chunk_results = pool.map(build_posting_list, chunk_args)\n            posting_results.extend(chunk_results)\n\n        # Merge posting lists\n        for term, posting_list in posting_results:\n            index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n        # Step 5: Compute document vectors and norms in parallel\n        doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n        doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n        doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n        vector_args = [(doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf) for doc_chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n        vector_results = pool.map(compute_doc_vector_chunk, vector_args)\n\n        # Merge vector results\n        for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vector_results:\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n    finally:\n        pool.close()\n        pool.join()\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries: list[str], index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int = 10, num_workers=None, documents=None) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Batch queries to reduce multiprocessing overhead\n    if len(queries) <= num_workers:\n        # If we have fewer queries than workers, use one query per worker\n        search_args = [(query, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]\n        with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n            results = pool.map(search_single_query, search_args)\n    else:\n        # Batch queries - process multiple queries per worker\n        batch_size = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n        query_batches = [queries[i:i + batch_size] for i in range(0, len(queries), batch_size)]\n\n        search_args = [(query_batch, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query_batch in query_batches]\n\n        with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n            batch_results = pool.map(search_batch_queries, search_args)\n\n        # Flatten batch results\n        results = []\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            results.extend(batch)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of worker processes\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    from sequential import load_corpus, generate_corpus\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Build index\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index (parallel)...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.workers, chunk_size=500)\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform search\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    queries = [args.query, \"machine learning\", \"deep learning\", \"neural networks\"]\n    results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(queries, result.index, args.top_k, num_workers=args.workers, documents=documents)\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(results[0])} results for '{queries[0]}':\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(results[0], 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. [{res.score:.4f}] {res.title} (doc_id: {res.doc_id})\")\n\n    return result\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":371,"oldLines":8,"newStart":371,"newLines":12,"lines":["     if len(queries) <= num_workers:","         # If we have fewer queries than workers, use one query per worker","         search_args = [(query, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]","-        with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:","+        pool = mp.Pool(processes=num_workers)","+        try:","             results = pool.map(search_single_query, search_args)","+        finally:","+            pool.close()","+            pool.join()","     else:","         # Batch queries - process multiple queries per worker","         batch_size = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)"]},{"oldStart":380,"oldLines":8,"newStart":384,"newLines":12,"lines":[" ","         search_args = [(query_batch, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query_batch in query_batches]"," ","-        with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:","+        pool = mp.Pool(processes=num_workers)","+        try:","             batch_results = pool.map(search_batch_queries, search_args)","+        finally:","+            pool.close()","+            pool.join()"," ","         # Flatten batch results","         results = []"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Use more efficient data structures\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport multiprocessing as mp\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict, Counter\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: ============================================================================\n\n\ndef process_documents_worker(documents):\n    \"\"\"Process a batch of documents and return partial results.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of documents to process\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs_dict, vocabulary_set, doc_terms_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    vocabulary = doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary, doc_terms)\n\n\ndef compute_df_worker(args):\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a subset of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_list, doc_terms_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary of term -> document frequency\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_list, doc_terms = args\n    df_dict = {}\n    for term in terms_list:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        df_dict[term] = df\n    return df_dict\n\n\ndef build_index_worker(args):\n    \"\"\"Build inverted index and document vectors for a subset of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf, build_inverted)\n\n    Returns:\n        If build_inverted=True: (inverted_index_dict, doc_vectors_dict, doc_norms_dict)\n        If build_inverted=False: (doc_vectors_dict, doc_norms_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf, build_inverted = args\n\n    if build_inverted:\n        inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n        doc_vectors = {}\n        doc_norms = {}\n\n        # First, collect all (term, doc_id, tfidf) triples\n        for doc_id in doc_ids:\n            if doc_id not in doc_term_freqs:\n                continue\n\n            tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n            norm_squared = 0.0\n            doc_vector = {}\n\n            for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n                if term in idf:\n                    tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                    doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                    norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n                    inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n            doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n        # Sort posting lists\n        for term in inverted_index:\n            inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n        return (dict(inverted_index), doc_vectors, doc_norms)\n    else:\n        doc_vectors = {}\n        doc_norms = {}\n\n        for norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n            doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n            doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n        return (doc_vectors, doc_norms)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents: list[Document], num_workers=None, chunk_size=2000) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    This version processes documents in larger batches to reduce overhead.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per worker (default: 2000)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Process documents in parallel\n    print(f\"Step 1: Processing {len(documents)} documents with {num_workers} workers...\")\n    n_chunks = min(num_workers, (len(documents) + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size)\n    chunks = [documents[i::n_chunks] for i in range(n_chunks)]\n\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(process_documents_worker, chunks)\n\n    # Merge results\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_vocab, chunk_doc_terms in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n        vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 2: Compute document frequencies in parallel\n    print(f\"Step 2: Computing document frequencies for {len(vocabulary)} terms...\")\n    vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n    n_vocab_chunks = min(num_workers * 2, (len(vocabulary_list) + 99) // 100)\n    vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i::n_vocab_chunks] for i in range(n_vocab_chunks)]\n\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(compute_df_worker, [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in vocab_chunks])\n\n    # Merge DF results\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    print(\"Step 3: Computing IDF scores...\")\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build inverted index and document vectors in parallel\n    print(\"Step 4: Building inverted index and document vectors...\")\n    idf = index.idf\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    n_doc_chunks = min(num_workers * 2, (len(doc_ids) + 99) // 100)\n    doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i::n_doc_chunks] for i in range(n_doc_chunks)]\n\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        index_results = pool.map(\n            build_index_worker,\n            [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf, True) for chunk in doc_chunks]\n        )\n\n    # Merge index results\n    for inverted_index_part, doc_vectors_part, doc_norms_part in index_results:\n        index.inverted_index.update(inverted_index_part)\n        index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors_part)\n        index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms_part)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef search_query_worker(args):\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index_dict, top_k, doc_titles_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Convert index to TFIDFIndex-like object\n    class SimpleIndex:\n        def __init__(self, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms):\n            self.idf = idf\n            self.inverted_index = inverted_index\n            self.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n            self.doc_norms = doc_norms\n\n    simple_index = SimpleIndex(\n        idf=index['idf'],\n        inverted_index=index['inverted_index'],\n        doc_vectors=index['doc_vectors'],\n        doc_norms=index['doc_norms']\n    )\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in simple_index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * simple_index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in simple_index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in simple_index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = simple_index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = simple_index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries: list[str], index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int = 10, num_workers=None, documents=None) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    # Prepare index for serialization\n    index_dict = {\n        'idf': index.idf,\n        'inverted_index': index.inverted_index,\n        'doc_vectors': index.doc_vectors,\n        'doc_norms': index.doc_norms\n    }\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Process queries\n    with mp.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(\n            search_query_worker,\n            [(query, index_dict, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]\n        )\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of worker processes\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    from sequential import load_corpus, generate_corpus\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine (v2)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for terms in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores in parallel\n    5. Compute document vector norms in parallel\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 1000)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Create pool once and reuse\n    pool = mp.Pool(processes=num_workers)\n\n    try:\n        # Step 1: Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n        chunks = [documents[i:i + chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size)]\n        chunk_args = [(chunk, i) for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)]\n\n        results = pool.map(process_document_chunk, chunk_args)\n\n        # Merge results from all chunks\n        doc_term_freqs = {}\n        doc_terms = {}\n        vocabulary = set()\n\n        for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_doc_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n            vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n        index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n        # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n        vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n        vocab_chunk_size = max(100, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n        vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i:i + vocab_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size)]\n        df_args = [(vocab_chunk, doc_terms) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n        df_results = pool.map(compute_document_frequencies, df_args)\n\n        # Merge DF results\n        for df_dict in df_results:\n            index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n        # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast operation)\n        N = index.num_documents\n        for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n            index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n        # Step 4: Build inverted index in parallel\n        idf = index.idf\n        # Process terms in larger chunks to reduce overhead\n        terms_list = list(vocabulary)\n        term_chunk_size = max(50, len(terms_list) // (num_workers * 4))\n        term_chunks = [terms_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(terms_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n        posting_results = []\n        for term_chunk in term_chunks:\n            chunk_args = [(term, doc_term_freqs, idf[term]) for term in term_chunk]\n            chunk_results = pool.map(build_posting_list, chunk_args)\n            posting_results.extend(chunk_results)\n\n        # Merge posting lists\n        for term, posting_list in posting_results:\n            index.inverted_index[term] = posting_list\n\n        # Step 5: Compute document vectors and norms in parallel\n        doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n        doc_chunk_size = max(100, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n        doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n        vector_args = [(doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf) for doc_chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n        vector_results = pool.map(compute_doc_vector_chunk, vector_args)\n\n        # Merge vector results\n        for doc_vectors, doc_norms in vector_results:\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n    finally:\n        pool.close()\n        pool.join()\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for terms in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores in parallel\n    5. Compute document vector norms in parallel\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 1500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = mp.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Use fewer Pool operations with larger chunks to reduce overhead\n    pool = mp.Pool(processes=num_workers)\n\n    try:\n        # Step 1: Process documents in parallel (larger chunks)\n        n_chunks = min(num_workers, (len(documents) + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size)\n        if n_chunks == 0:\n            n_chunks = 1\n        chunks = [documents[i::n_chunks] for i in range(n_chunks)]\n        chunk_args = [(chunk, i) for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)]\n\n        results = pool.map(process_document_chunk, chunk_args)\n\n        # Merge results\n        doc_term_freqs = {}\n        doc_terms = {}\n        vocabulary = set()\n\n        for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_doc_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n            doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n            doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n            vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n        index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n        # Step 2: Compute document frequencies in parallel\n        vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n        # Use larger chunks to reduce overhead\n        vocab_chunk_size = max(200, len(vocabulary_list) // num_workers if num_workers > 0 else len(vocabulary_list))\n        vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i:i + vocab_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size)]\n        df_args = [(vocab_chunk, doc_terms) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n        df_results = pool.map(compute_document_frequencies, df_args)\n\n        # Merge DF results\n        for df_dict in df_results:\n            index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n        # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n        N = index.num_documents\n        for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n            index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n        # Step 4 & 5: Build inverted index and document vectors in parallel\n        idf = index.idf\n        doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n        # Use larger chunks\n        doc_chunk_size = max(200, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2) if num_workers > 0 else len(doc_ids))\n        if doc_chunk_size == 0:\n            doc_chunk_size = 1\n        doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n        vector_args = [(doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf) for doc_chunk in doc_chunks]\n\n        vector_results = pool.map(compute_doc_vector_chunk, vector_args)\n\n        # Merge results and build inverted index\n        for doc_chunk in doc_chunks:\n            # Get results for this chunk\n            chunk_result = None\n            for vr in vector_results:\n                # Check if this result matches the chunk\n                if len(vr[0]) == len(doc_chunk):\n                    chunk_result = vr\n                    break\n\n            if chunk_result:\n                doc_vectors, doc_norms = chunk_result\n\n                # Update index\n                index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n                index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n                # Build inverted index for terms in these documents\n                for doc_id in doc_chunk:\n                    if doc_id in doc_vectors:\n                        for term, tfidf_score in doc_vectors[doc_id].items():\n                            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n                            index.inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf_score))\n\n        # Sort posting lists\n        for term in index.inverted_index:\n            index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    finally:\n        pool.close()\n        pool.join()\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":740,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1267,"cache_read_input_tokens":54337},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8b6d14d9-3833-48e0-93e2-66fec3c6b7e6","uuid":"7c7b49ab-c623-46dd-ac36-3a516dc633c7"}
