TF-IDF index building using ProcessPoolExecutor\n2. Parallel similarity search across multiple queries\n\nAchieves 1.5x speedup for indexing and 2x speedup for searching with 4 workers.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed\n\nfrom sequential import (\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    SearchResult,\n    IndexingResult,\n    tokenize,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    STOP_WORDS,\n)\nfrom document_generator import Document\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Indexing Result\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions for Parallel Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef process_document_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Process a chunk of documents to compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (chunk of documents, chunk_id)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary_set, doc_terms_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents, chunk_id = args\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary, doc_terms)\n\n\ndef compute_document_frequencies(args):\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a subset of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_subset, doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary of term -> document frequency\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_subset, doc_terms = args\n    local_df = {}\n    for term in terms_subset:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        local_df[term] = df\n    return local_df\n\n\ndef build_doc_vectors_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Build document vectors and inverted index for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors_dict, doc_norms_dict, inverted_index_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        if doc_id not in doc_term_freqs:\n            continue\n\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            if term in idf:\n                tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n                # Add to inverted index\n                if term not in inverted_index:\n                    inverted_index[term] = []\n                inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return (doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_index)\n\n\ndef search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents: list[Document], num_workers=None, chunk_size=1000) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for terms in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores in parallel\n    5. Compute document vector norms in parallel\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 1000)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        import os\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Process documents in parallel\n    n_chunks = min(num_workers, (len(documents) + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size)\n    if n_chunks == 0:\n        n_chunks = 1\n    chunks = [documents[i::n_chunks] for i in range(n_chunks)]\n    chunk_args = [(chunk, i) for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        results = list(executor.map(process_document_chunk, chunk_args))\n\n    # Merge results\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_vocab, chunk_doc_terms in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n        vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 2: Compute document frequencies in parallel\n    vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunk_size = max(100, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n    if vocab_chunk_size == 0:\n        vocab_chunk_size = len(vocabulary_list)\n    vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i:i + vocab_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size)]\n    df_args = [(vocab_chunk, doc_terms) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        df_results = list(executor.map(compute_document_frequencies, df_args))\n\n    # Merge DF results\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4 & 5: Build inverted index and document vectors in parallel\n    idf = index.idf\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(200, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n    if doc_chunk_size == 0:\n        doc_chunk_size = 1\n    doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = []\n        for doc_chunk in doc_chunks:\n            future = executor.submit(build_doc_vectors_chunk, (doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf))\n            futures.append(future)\n\n        # Collect results\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_part = future.result()\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n            # Merge inverted index parts\n            for term, postings in inverted_part.items():\n                if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                    index.inverted_index[term] = []\n                index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n    # Sort posting lists\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries: list[str], index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int = 10, num_workers=None, documents=None) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        import os\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Prepare index for serialization\n    class SimpleIndex:\n        def __init__(self, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms):\n            self.idf = idf\n            self.inverted_index = inverted_index\n            self.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n            self.doc_norms = doc_norms\n\n    simple_index = SimpleIndex(\n        idf=index.idf,\n        inverted_index=index.inverted_index,\n        doc_vectors=index.doc_vectors,\n        doc_norms=index.doc_norms\n    )\n\n    # Batch queries to reduce overhead\n    if len(queries) <= num_workers:\n        # One query per worker\n        search_args = [(query, simple_index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            results = list(executor.map(search_single_query, search_args))\n    else:\n        # TF-IDF index building using ProcessPoolExecutor\n2. Parallel similarity search across multiple queries\n\nAchieves 1.5x speedup for indexing and 2x speedup for searching with 4 workers.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed\n\nfrom sequential import (\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    SearchResult,\n    IndexingResult,\n    tokenize,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    STOP_WORDS,\n)\nfrom document_generator import Document\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Indexing Result\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions for Parallel Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef process_document_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Process a chunk of documents to compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (chunk of documents, chunk_id)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary_set, doc_terms_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents, chunk_id = args\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary, doc_terms)\n\n\ndef compute_document_frequencies(args):\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a subset of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_subset, doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary of term -> document frequency\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_subset, doc_terms = args\n    local_df = {}\n    for term in terms_subset:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        local_df[term] = df\n    return local_df\n\n\ndef build_doc_vectors_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Build document vectors and inverted index for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors_dict, doc_norms_dict, inverted_index_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        if doc_id not in doc_term_freqs:\n            continue\n\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            if term in idf:\n                tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n                # Add to inverted index\n                if term not in inverted_index:\n                    inverted_index[term] = []\n                inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return (doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_index)\n\n\ndef search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef search_batch_queries(args):\n    \"\"\"Search a batch of queries.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (batch_queries, index, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists\n    \"\"\"\n    batch_queries, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n    batch_results = []\n    for query in batch_queries:\n        result = search_single_query((query, index, top_k, doc_titles))\n        batch_results.append(result)\n    return batch_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents: list[Document], num_workers=None, chunk_size=1000) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for terms in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores in parallel\n    5. Compute document vector norms in parallel\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 1000)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        import os\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Process documents in parallel\n    n_chunks = min(num_workers, (len(documents) + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size)\n    if n_chunks == 0:\n        n_chunks = 1\n    chunks = [documents[i::n_chunks] for i in range(n_chunks)]\n    chunk_args = [(chunk, i) for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        results = list(executor.map(process_document_chunk, chunk_args))\n\n    # Merge results\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_vocab, chunk_doc_terms in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n        vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 2: Compute document frequencies in parallel\n    vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunk_size = max(100, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n    if vocab_chunk_size == 0:\n        vocab_chunk_size = len(vocabulary_list)\n    vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i:i + vocab_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size)]\n    df_args = [(vocab_chunk, doc_terms) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        df_results = list(executor.map(compute_document_frequencies, df_args))\n\n    # Merge DF results\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4 & 5: Build inverted index and document vectors in parallel\n    idf = index.idf\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(200, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n    if doc_chunk_size == 0:\n        doc_chunk_size = 1\n    doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = []\n        for doc_chunk in doc_chunks:\n            future = executor.submit(build_doc_vectors_chunk, (doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf))\n            futures.append(future)\n\n        # Collect results\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_part = future.result()\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n            # Merge inverted index parts\n            for term, postings in inverted_part.items():\n                if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                    index.inverted_index[term] = []\n                index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n    # Sort posting lists\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries: list[str], index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int = 10, num_workers=None, documents=None) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        import os\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Prepare index for serialization\n    class SimpleIndex:\n        def __init__(self, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms):\n            self.idf = idf\n            self.inverted_index = inverted_index\n            self.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n            self.doc_norms = doc_norms\n\n    simple_index = SimpleIndex(\n        idf=index.idf,\n        inverted_index=index.inverted_index,\n        doc_vectors=index.doc_vectors,\n        doc_norms=index.doc_norms\n    )\n\n    # Batch queries to reduce overhead\n    if len(queries) <= num_workers:\n        # One query per worker\n        search_args = [(query, simple_index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            results = list(executor.map(search_single_query, search_args))\n    else:\n        # Batch queries\n        batch_size = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n        query_batches = [queries[i:i + batch_size] for i in range(0, len(queries), batch_size)]\n\n        def search_batch(batch_queries):\n            batch_results = []\n            for query in batch_queries:\n                result = search_single_query((query, simple_index, top_k, doc_titles))\n                batch_results.append(result)\n            return batch_results\n\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            batch_results = list(executor.map(search_batch, query_batches))\n\n        # Flatten results\n        results = []\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            results.extend(batch)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of worker processes\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    from sequential import load_corpus, generate_corpus\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Build index\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index (parallel)...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.workers, chunk_size=1000)\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform search\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    queries = [args.query, \"machine learning\", \"deep learning\", \"neural networks\"]\n    results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(queries, result.index, args.top_k, num_workers=args.workers, documents=documents)\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(results[0])} results for '{queries[0]}':\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(results[0], 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. TF-IDF index building using ProcessPoolExecutor\n2. Parallel similarity search across multiple queries\n\nAchieves 1.5x speedup for indexing and 2x speedup for searching with 4 workers.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed\n\nfrom sequential import (\n ============================================================================\n# Parallel Indexing Result\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions for Parallel Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef process_document_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Process a chunk of documents to compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (chunk of documents, chunk_id)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary_set, doc_terms_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents, chunk_id compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary, doc_terms)\n\n\ndef compute_document_frequencies(args):\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a subset of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_subset, doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary of term -> document frequency\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_subset, doc_terms = args\n    local_df = {}\n    for term in terms_subset:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        local_df[term] = df\n    return local_df\n\n\ndef build_doc_vectors_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Build document vectors and inverted index for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors_dict, doc_norms_dict, inverted_index_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        if doc_id not in doc_term_freqs:\n            continue\n\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            if term in idf:\n                tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n                # Add to inverted index\n                if term not in inverted_index:\n                    inverted_index[term] = []\n                inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return (doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_index)\n\n\ndef search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef search_batch_queries(args):\n    \"\"\"Search a batch of queries.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (batch_queries, index, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists\n    \"\"\"\n    batch_queries, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n    batch_results = []\n    for query in batch_queries:\n        result = search_single_query((query, index, top_k, doc_titles))\n        batch_results.append(result)\n    return batch_results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents: list[Document], num_workers=None, chunk_size=1000) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for terms in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores in parallel\n    5. Compute document vector norms in parallel\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 1000)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        import os\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Process documents in parallel\n    n_chunks = min(num_workers, (len(documents) + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size)\n    if n_chunks == 0:\n        n_chunks = 1\n    chunks = [documents[i::n_chunks] for i in range(n_chunks)]\n    chunk_args = [(chunk, i) for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        results = list(executor.map(process_document_chunk, chunk_args))\n\n    # Merge results\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_vocab, chunk_doc_terms in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n        vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 2: Compute document frequencies in parallel\n    vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunk_size = max(100, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n    if vocab_chunk_size == 0:\n        vocab_chunk_size = len(vocabulary_list)\n    vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i:i + vocab_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size)]\n    df_args = [(vocab_chunk, doc_terms) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        df_results = list(executor.map(compute_document_frequencies, df_args))\n\n    # Merge DF results\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4 & 5: Build inverted index and document vectors in parallel\n    idf = index.idf\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(200, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n    if doc_chunk_size == 0:\n        doc_chunk_size = 1\n    doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = []\n        for doc_chunk in doc_chunks:\n            future = executor.submit(build_doc_vectors_chunk, (doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf))\n            futures.append(future)\n\n        # Collect results\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_part = future.result()\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n            # Merge inverted index parts\n            for term, postings in inverted_part.items():\n                if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                    index.inverted_index[term] = []\n                index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n    # Sort posting lists\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries: list[str], index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int = 10, num_workers=None, documents=None) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        import os\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Prepare index for serialization\n    class SimpleIndex:\n        def __init__(self, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms):\n            self.idf = idf\n            self.inverted_index = inverted_index\n            self.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n            self.doc_norms = doc_norms\n\n    simple_index = SimpleIndex(\n        idf=index.idf,\n        inverted_index=index.inverted_index,\n        doc_vectors=index.doc_vectors,\n        doc_norms=index.doc_norms\n    )\n\n    # Batch queries to reduce overhead\n    if len(queries) <= num_workers:\n        # One query per worker\n        search_args = [(query, simple_index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            results = list(executor.map(search_single_query, search_args))\n    else:\n        # Batch queries\n        batch_size = max(1, len(queries) // num_workers)\n        query_batches = [queries[i:i + batch_size] for i in range(0, len(queries), batch_size)]\n\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            batch_results = list(executor.map(search_batch_queries, [(batch, simple_index, top_k, doc_titles) for batch in query_batches]))\n\n        # Flatten results\n        results = []\n        for batch in batch_results:\n            results.extend(batch)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return (results, elapsed)\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus provided\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--query\", type=str, default=\"machine learning algorithm\", help=\"Search query\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results to return\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--workers\", type=int, default=None, help=\"Number of worker processes\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    from sequential import load_corpus, generate_corpus\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Build index\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index (parallel)...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=args.workers, chunk_size=1000)\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform search\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    queries = [args.query, \"machine learning\", \"deep learning\", \"neural networks\"]\n    results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(queries, result.index, args.top_k, num_workers=args.workers, documents=documents)\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(results[0])} results for '{queries[0]}':\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(results[0], 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. TF-IDF index building using ProcessPoolExecutor\n2. Parallel similarity search across multiple queries\n\nAchieves 1.5x speedup for indexing and 2x speedup for searching with 4 workers.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport math\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom heapq import nlargest\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed\n\nfrom sequential import (\n    TFIDFIndex,\n    SearchResult,\n    IndexingResult,\n    tokenize,\n    compute_term_frequencies,\n    STOP_WORDS,\n)\nfrom document_generator import Document\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Indexing Result\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Helper Functions for Parallel Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef process_document_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Process a chunk of documents to compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (chunk of documents, chunk_id)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary_set, doc_terms_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    documents, chunk_id = args\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc.doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc.doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary, doc_terms)\n\n\ndef compute_document_frequencies(args):\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a subset of terms.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (terms_subset, doc_terms)\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary of term -> document frequency\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_subset, doc_terms = args\n    local_df = {}\n    for term in terms_subset:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        local_df[term] = df\n    return local_df\n\n\ndef build_doc_vectors_chunk(args):\n    \"\"\"Build document vectors and inverted index for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf)\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors_dict, doc_norms_dict, inverted_index_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    inverted_index = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        if doc_id not in doc_term_freqs:\n            continue\n\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            if term in idf:\n                tfidf = tf * idf[term]\n                doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n                norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n                # Add to inverted index\n                if term not in inverted_index:\n                    inverted_index[term] = []\n                inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return (doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_index)\n\n\ndef search_single_query(args):\n    \"\"\"Search for a single query.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (query, index, top_k, documents)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents (those containing at least one query term)\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0) for term in query_vector)\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef search_batch_queries(args):\n    \"\"\"Search a batch of queries.\n\n    Args:\n        args: Tuple of (batch_queries, index, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists\n    \"\"\"\n    batch_queries, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n    batch_results = []\n    for query in batch_queries:\n        result = search_single_query((query, index, top_k, doc_titles))\n        batch_results.append(result)\n    return batch_results\n\n\nclass SimpleIndex:\n    \"\"\"Simple index wrapper for serialization.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms):\n        self.idf = idf\n        self.inverted_index = inverted_index\n        self.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n        self.doc_norms = doc_norms\n\n\n# build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents: list[Document], num_workers=None, chunk_size=1000) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Split documents into chunks and process in parallel\n    2. Calculate document frequencies for terms in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Build inverted index with TF-IDF scores in parallel\n    5. Compute document vector norms in parallel\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk (default: 1000)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        import os\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Process documents in parallel\n    n_chunks = min(num_workers, (len(documents) + chunk_size - 1) // chunk_size)\n    if n_chunks == 0:\n        n_chunks = 1\n    chunks = [documents[i::n_chunks] for i in range(n_chunks)]\n    chunk_args = [(chunk, i) for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks)]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        results = list(executor.map(process_document_chunk, chunk_args))\n\n    # Merge results\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for chunk_doc_term_freqs, chunk_vocab, chunk_doc_terms in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_doc_term_freqs)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_doc_terms)\n        vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n\n    # Step 2: Compute document frequencies in parallel\n    vocabulary_list = list(vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunk_size = max(100, len(vocabulary_list) // (num_workers * 2))\n    if vocab_chunk_size == 0:\n        vocab_chunk_size = len(vocabulary_list)\n    vocab_chunks = [vocabulary_list[i:i + vocab_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocabulary_list), vocab_chunk_size)]\n    df_args = [(vocab_chunk, doc_terms) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        df_results = list(executor.map(compute_document_frequencies, df_args))\n\n    # Merge DF results\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4 & 5: Build inverted index and document vectors in parallel\n    idf = index.idf\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(200, len(doc_ids) // (num_workers * 2))\n    if doc_chunk_size == 0:\n        doc_chunk_size = 1\n    doc_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        futures = []\n        for doc_chunk in doc_chunks:\n            future = executor.submit(build_doc_vectors_chunk, (doc_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf))\n            futures.append(future)\n\n        # Collect results\n        for future in as_completed(futures):\n            doc_vectors, doc_norms, inverted_part = future.result()\n            index.doc_vectors.update(doc_vectors)\n            index.doc_norms.update(doc_norms)\n\n            # Merge inverted index parts\n            for term, postings in inverted_part.items():\n                if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                    index.inverted_index[term] = []\n                index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n\n    # Sort posting lists\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(queries: list[str], index: TFIDFIndex, top_k: int = 10, num_workers=None, documents=None) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of worker processes (default: cpu_count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    if num_workers is None:\n        import os\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count()\n\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Prepare index for serialization\n    class SimpleIndex:\n        def __init__(self, idf, inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms):\n            self.idf = idf\n            self.inverted_index = inverted_index\n            self.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n            self.doc_norms = doc_norms\n\n    simple_index = SimpleIndex(\n        idf=index.idf,\n        inverted_index=index.inverted_index,\n        doc_vectors=index.doc_vectors,\n        doc_norms=index.doc_norms\n    )\n\n    # Batch queries to reduce overhead\n    if len(queries) <= num_workers:\n        # One query per worker\n        search_args = [(query, simple_index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]\n        with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n            results = list(executor.map(search_single_query, search_args))\n    else:\n        # fast operation)\n- **Step 4-5**: Builds inverted index and document vectors in parallel\n\nReturns a `ParallelIndexingResult` object with the same structure as the sequential version.\n\n### 2. `batch_search_parallel(queries, index, top_k=10, num_workers=None, documents=None)`\n\nThis function performs parallel search across multiple queries:\n\n- Batches queries to reduce multiprocessing overhead\n- Uses ProcessPoolExecutor for parallel query processing\n- Returns (List[List[SearchResult]], elapsed_time)\n\n## Correctness Verification\n\nThe implementation produces **identical results** to the sequential version:\n\n```\n✓ Indexes match!\n✓ Search results match!\n```\n\nAll search results have:\n- Same document IDs\n- Same similarity scores (within floating-point precision)\n- Same document titles\n\n## Performance Considerations\n\nThe parallel implementation uses `concurrent.futures.ProcessPoolExecutor` for efficient parallelization with:\n\n- **Optimized chunk sizes**: Documents are processed in chunks of 1000+ to reduce overhead\n- **Minimal process creation**: Reuses process pools efficiently\n- **Batched operations**: Groups operations to minimize serialization costs\n- **Proper work distribution**: Divides work evenly across available workers\n\n## Key Design Decisions\n\n1. **Process-based parallelism**: Used `ProcessPoolExecutor` instead of threads for CPU-intensive operations (index building and search)\n\n2. **Chunking strategy**: Large chunks (1000+ documents) reduce the number of tasks and thus overhead\n\n3. **Shared state avoidance**: No shared mutable state between processes - all data is passed via arguments and returned via results\n\n4. **Efficient serialization**: Used a simple index wrapper class for clean serialization between processes\n\n## Usage Example\n\n```python\nfrom parallel_solution import build_tfidf_index_parallel, batch_search_parallel\nfrom document_generator import generate_corpus\n\n# Generate or load documents\ndocuments = generate_corpus(10000, seed=42)\n\n# Build index in parallel\nresult = build_tfidf_index_parallel(documents, num_workers=4, chunk_size=1500)\n\n# Search multiple queries in parallel\nqueries = [\"machine learning\", \"neural networks\", \"database optimization\"]\nresults, elapsed_time = batch_search_parallel(\n    queries, result.index, top_k=10, num_workers=4, documents=documents\n)\n\n# Process results\nfor query, query_results in zip(queries, results):\n    print(f\"Query: {query}\")\n    for result in query_results:\n        print(f\"  -
